Game downloadsPS3 themes

whatwhatboy / PLAYSTATION 3

PS3 console file list

842 PS3 files. Plain links work without JavaScript. Packages, game files and archives remain separate.

PS3 hubGame modsPKGMod filesArchivesPC helpers

Download is the first step. A PKG still needs installation through the supported PS3 environment. An SPRX needs its game-specific loader and destination. ZIP/RAR/7z files need extraction. These files do not establish compatibility with an unmodified PS3.

Modern HTTPS or file hosts may fail in the PS3 browser. Use a direct file URL from your own HTTP LAN server, USB/FTP, or a compatible PS3 file client. Keep the original archives and readmes.

Large PKGs in your local archive: the PS3-only LAN archive server offers direct downloads of larger builds such as the GTA IV texture installer and Uncharted installer. Set up archive downloads over HTTP.

Optional webMAN download links

Requires webMAN MOD already running on the PS3. These links save to /dev_hdd0/packages; they do not install, extract or launch files. From the PS3, use 127.0.0.1. From another device, enter the PS3’s LAN IP.

JavaScript is optional. Set the addresses before using a webMAN link.
